# Functional requirements

| ID | Requirement | URS | Design | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FRS-BB-001 | Patient identity match at issue requires two tokens that match stored MRN/DOB/name fields (`PatientIdentityMatchRule`). | URS-BB-001 | `IssueGate` `ISS-IDENTITY` | Domain + issue integration |
| FRS-BB-002 | Antibody history is append-only. Add requires `immuno.record`; deactivation requires a reason and `immuno.override` in the Application service. Deactivated rows remain visible and continue to block electronic XM. | URS-BB-002 | `AntibodyHistory`; `ImmunoAuthorizationRule`; `ElectronicCrossmatchEligibilityService` | `ElectronicXmHistoryRegressionTests`; Phase 3 antibody permission tests |
| FRS-BB-003 | Merged patient records HardStop accession, visits, orders, result entry/verify/correct/invalidate, immunohematology writes, special requirements, issue, allocation, compatibility evaluation, and crossmatch (`PAT-MERGED-INACTIVE`). Inactive status is not blocked. Staff with `patient.merge` can merge a duplicate into the survivor (`POST /api/patients/{id}/merge` and ADT A18/A40). Demographic `patient.write` does not grant merge. History is reassigned, not deleted. Antibody-identification workups are reassigned to the survivor; merge HardStops if both records have an open workup (`ABID-MERGE-DUP-OPEN`) and warns if one does (`ABID-MERGE-WORKUP`). Discordant ABO/Rh HardStops the merge. | URS-BB-015, URS-BB-017 | `PatientMergeService`; `PatientMergeRule`; patient detail merge UI | `PatientMergeServiceTests`; Phase 8 seed tests; Phase 5 HL7 merge tests; `MergedPatientClinicalUseTests` |
| FRS-BB-010 | Specimen expiration is computed from collection time and alloimmunization-risk policy. Expired or non-accepted specimens HardStop issue, result entry, and result verification. | URS-BB-003, URS-BB-016 | `SpecimenExpirationRule`; `ResultService.ValidateSpecimenForEntryAsync` | Domain expiry boundary; `MergedPatientClinicalUseTests` |
| FRS-BB-020 | Verified results are not overwritten. Corrections create a new version and retain the original. | URS-BB-004 | `ResultService` | Result versioning tests |
| FRS-BB-079 | Result provenance is stored as Manual, Instrument, Interface, or Calculated. Instrument and interface entries start `PendingVerification`. Manual and calculated entries start `Entered` and may be submitted for verification. | URS-BB-047 | `ResultSource`; `ResultLifecycleRule`; `ResultService` | `ResultLifecycleRuleTests`; `ResultLifecycleTests` |
| FRS-BB-089 | ABO/Rh panel entry and catalog-interpretation saves set `ResultSource.Calculated` unless the observation already arrived as Instrument or Interface. Test-definition create, update, and clone write `AuditEventType.TestChange` (activate/deactivate remain Activate/Deactivate). | URS-BB-047, URS-BB-056, URS-BB-011 | `ResultProvenanceRule`; `ResultService`; `TestDefinitionAdminService` | `ResultProvenanceRuleTests`; `ResultLifecycleTests`; `AdminConfigTests` |
| FRS-BB-082 | Inbound ORU posts through `ResultService.EnterFromInterfaceAsync`. Source is Interface; status is `PendingVerification`. Verified rows are not overwritten. The order must belong to the PID patient and have a linked accepted specimen. OBX-11 empty/F/C/P/R may post; other statuses NAK (OCD-019). | URS-BB-049 | `Hl7OruMapper`; `InterfaceResultAcceptanceRule`; `Hl7InboundProcessor` | `InterfaceResultAcceptanceRuleTests`; `Hl7MapperTests`; `Phase5Hl7Tests` inbound ORU |
| FRS-BB-077 | Invalidating a result requires `result.invalidate` and a reason. A verified result is superseded by a new Invalidated version; the original row stays Verified. An unverified correction is marked Invalidated and the prior verified row becomes current again (OCD-016). Posted ABO/antibody history is not auto-reverted (OCD-017). | URS-BB-046 | `ResultService.InvalidateResultAsync`; `ResultAuthorizationRule` | `ResultLifecycleTests`; `ResultAuthorizationRuleTests` |
| FRS-BB-021 | Manually recording current ABO/Rh requires `immuno.override` in the Application service (not only the API). Antibody add and antigen-profile writes require `immuno.record`. Antibody deactivate requires `immuno.override`. A reason is required for manual ABO and deactivate. History is append-only. Changing ABO/Rh while an antibody-identification workup is open warns (`ABID-ABO-OPEN`) and withdraws interpretation and review; the same type does not withdraw. Manual ABO after a completed unscoped workup warns (`ABID-ABO-DONE`) and does not reopen or retract posted history. | URS-BB-019, URS-BB-002 | `ImmunohematologyService`; `ImmunoAuthorizationRule`; `AntibodyIdentificationHistoryPostRule` | `ImmunoAuthorizationRuleTests`; Phase 3 immuno permission tests; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.RecordBloodType_OpenWorkup_WarnsAndWithdrawsInterpretation`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.RecordBloodType_AfterCompletedUnscopedWorkup_WarnsAndDoesNotReopen` |
| FRS-BB-022 | Patient ABO/Rh verification applies `SelfVerifyRule` when `Result.BlockAboSelfVerify` is on (default). `MarkComplete` does not auto-verify ABO/Rh. Current `PatientBloodTypeHistory` is written only after a successful verify. A verified type change while an antibody-identification workup is open warns (`ABID-ABO-OPEN`) and withdraws interpretation and review; Retain and the same type do not withdraw. Correcting a verified ABO/Rh while a workup is open warns (`ABID-TYPE-CORR`) and does not withdraw; complete and supervisor Accept HardStop (`ABID-TYPE-PENDING`) until the correction is verified or invalidated. A type-changing ABO/Rh verify after a completed in-scope workup warns (`ABID-ABO-DONE`) and does not reopen or retract posted history. | URS-BB-020 | `ResultService`; `SelfVerifyRule`; `AntibodyIdentificationHistoryPostRule` | Phase 3 ABO self-verify and MarkComplete tests; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.VerifyAboRh_OpenWorkup_WarnsAndWithdrawsInterpretation`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.VerifyAboRh_AfterCompletedWorkup_SameSpecimen_WarnsAndDoesNotReopen`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.CorrectAntigen_OpenWorkup_WarnsAndDoesNotWithdraw` |
| FRS-BB-023 | Adding a special transfusion requirement requires `immuno.record` in the Application service. Deactivating one requires a reason and `immuno.override` and sets an end date. Callers that skip the API filter cannot change `ISS-SPECIAL-REQ` facts. Only clinically active rows (effective now, not end-dated) are listed as active and enforced. Adding or deactivating while an antibody-identification workup is open warns (`ABID-SR-OPEN`) and still records. Types come from the versioned `SpecialRequirementDefinitions` catalog (unit / issuing / patient). | URS-BB-021 | `SpecialRequirementService`; `SpecialRequirementAdminService`; `ImmunoAuthorizationRule`; `AntibodyIdentificationHistoryPostRule` | `ImmunoAuthorizationRuleTests`; Phase 3 special-requirement permission test; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.AddSpecialRequirement_OpenWorkup_WarnsAndAdds`; `SpecialRequirementAdminTests` |
| FRS-BB-024 | DIN lookback recall requires `lookback.manage` in the Application service. Each component recall is applied through `InventoryService.RecallForLookbackAsync`; a failed status transition fails the lookback. Selected/Assigned/Allocated/Crossmatched/ReturnPending may move to Recalled. Transfused units are notified, not recalled. | URS-BB-022 | `LookbackService`; `LookbackAuthorizationRule`; `InventoryStatusTransition` | `LookbackAuthorizationRuleTests`; `InventoryStatusTransitionTests`; Compliance lookback tests |
| FRS-BB-030 | Compatibility decisions return structured `RuleResult` codes and messages. The engine never downgrades a HardStop. | URS-BB-005 | `RuleEvaluation` | Aggregation tests |
| FRS-BB-031 | `IssueGate` evaluates identity, specimen, ABO/Rh, allocation, XM, special requirements, appearance, location, emergency ABO, antigen-negative, and autologous/directed rules before issue. | URS-BB-006 | `IssueGate` | `IssueGateSafetyRegressionTests` |
| FRS-BB-032 | Autologous/directed issue to a non-designated patient is HardStop `ISS-AUTO-DIR` inside the issue gate. | URS-BB-008 | `AutologousDirectedRule` | Domain + issue integration |
| FRS-BB-033 | Electronic XM eligibility is an evaluated service (`eligible` + criteria), not a checkbox. Facility policy is off by default (`XM-EC-POLICY`, OCD-006). Any antibody-history row fails `XM-EC-HISTORY`. An open antibody-identification workup fails `XM-EC-ABID-OPEN`; an active extended-crossmatch special requirement fails `XM-EC-EXTXM`. Serologic XM remains available and warns (`ABID-XM-OPEN`) without blocking. | URS-BB-009, URS-BB-189 | `ElectronicCrossmatchEligibilityService`; `ElectronicCrossmatchEligibilityRule`; `AntibodyIdentificationHistoryPostRule` | Eligibility tests; `CrossmatchRuleTests`; `Phase4IssuingTests.RecordCrossmatch_OpenAntibodyIdWorkup_WarnsAndRecords`; TEST-BB-074 |

Non-emergency warning overrides require `issue.override`. | URS-BB-006 | `IssuingService`; `IssueAuthorizationRule` | Phase 4 emergency tests; `IssueAuthorizationRuleTests`; Phase 8 seed tests |

| FRS-BB-095 | Antigen-profile save/update writes `AuditEventType.Antibody` with old/new (in-place overwrite, OCD-022). A result-sourced change without `immuno.override` HardStops `IH-AG-RESULT-LOCK` (FRS-BB-224). A phenotype change while an antibody-identification workup is open warns (`ABID-AG-OPEN`) and withdraws interpretation and review, including a verified AGTYPE result that changes the stored type. Correcting a verified antigen result while a workup is open warns (`ABID-TYPE-CORR`) and does not withdraw; complete and supervisor Accept HardStop (`ABID-TYPE-PENDING`) until the correction is verified or invalidated. A type-changing antigen verify after a completed same-specimen workup, a chart antigen save after a completed unscoped workup, or an antigen-positive type that conflicts with posted Identified history, warns (`ABID-AG-DONE`) and does not reopen or retract posted history. Reflex-added order lines from verified results write `AuditEventType.OrderChange` instead of Create. | URS-BB-061, URS-BB-011, URS-BB-190 | `ImmunohematologyService.SaveAntigenProfileAsync`; `ResultService.VerifyResultAsync`; `ResultService.CorrectResultAsync` | Phase 3 antigen named-audit test; TEST-BB-075; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.SaveAntigen_OpenWorkup_WarnsAndWithdrawsInterpretation`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.VerifyAntigen_OpenWorkup_WarnsAndWithdrawsInterpretation`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.CorrectAntigen_OpenWorkup_WarnsAndDoesNotWithdraw`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.VerifyAntigen_AfterCompletedWorkup_SameSpecimen_WarnsAndDoesNotReopen`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests.SaveAntigen_AfterCompletedUnscopedWorkup_WarnsAndDoesNotReopen`; `ReflexRulesTests.VerifyPositiveAbsc_AddsAbidLine_AndShowsOnWorklist` |

Due units still move to Expired; the sweep does not discard. | URS-BB-062 | `InventoryService.ExpireDueUnitsAsync`; `InventoryAuthorizationRule` | `InventoryAuthorizationRuleTests`; InventoryService expire permission test |
| FRS-BB-097 | Antibody-identification workups record manufacturer/lot/expiration, cells, antigen profiles, phases, strengths, autocontrol, DAT, selected cells, comments, assist findings, technologist interpretation, and supervisor review. Assist may emit Excluded/Possible/CannotExclude/Historical only — never Identified. Saving reactions, lots, DAT, or specimen scope refreshes stored assistance to the current antigram without identifying or posting. Completion HardStops without interpretation, on assist-Identified findings, and (default) without distinct supervisor acceptance. Changing panel reactions, selected-cell lots, DAT, specimen scope, ABO/Rh, or antigen type after interpretation withdraws interpretation and review (`ABID-INTERP-STALE` / `ABID-REVIEW-STALE`) and HardStops complete until both are repeated. The open workup names that withdrawal (panel, specimen, ABO/Rh, or antigen-type). Completing with Identified findings while a panel or selected cell lacks an interpretive-phase reaction HardStops (`ABID-INCOMPLETE-RXN`). Completing re-evaluates assistance and warns on leftover `CannotExclude` specificities (`ABID-UNEXCLUDED`) and homozygous selected-cell needs (`ABID-SEL-CELL`) without identifying them. Completing with no Identified findings warns (`ABID-COMPLETE-NONE`) and posts nothing. Clinical completion warnings require a technologist acknowledgment (`ABID-COMPLETE-ACK`) before history posts; acknowledgment does not identify antibodies. Completing while reserved or issued units remain on the patient warns (`ABID-COMPLETE-PRODUCTS`) and requires that acknowledgment; it does not identify antibodies or release the units. Voiding while reserved or issued units remain warns (`ABID-VOID-PRODUCTS`) and still voids without posting. Supervisor Accept HardStops Identified sign-off on an incomplete panel or a rejected/cancelled specimen and requires acknowledgment (`ABID-REVIEW-ACK`) for the same clinical warnings. Autocontrol reactive with DAT not performed warns (`ABID-DAT-INDICATED`) at complete and does not HardStop. History is posted only from technologist Identified findings after review. Identified specificities are resolved to the blood-attribute catalog when unique so they drive antigen-negative selection; unmatched Identified specificities warn and post as free-text. Identifying a catalog specificity when the patient is antigen-positive warns (`ABID-INTERP-PHENO` / `ABID-INTERP-GENO`) and does not HardStop. Identifying a specificity that assistance would exclude warns (`ABID-INTERP-EXCLUDED`) at interpret and complete and HardStops interpret without a rationale (`ABID-INTERP-EXCL-REASON`). Interpretation also warns on leftover `CannotExclude` (`ABID-UNEXCLUDED`), selected-cell need, history remaining, and incomplete reactions without identifying them. The patient Antibodies card, the patient workup list, and the `/antibody-id` open worklist show specimen accession or Unscoped, with patient MRN on the open worklist, flag withdrawn or clock-expired attached lots (`HasInactiveLot` / `HasExpiredLot`, with `InactiveLotNumbers` / `ExpiredLotNumbers`) including selected-cell reagents, list `AttachedLotNumbers` so a selected-cell lot is visible and filterable, expose manufacturer name/code (`AttachedManufacturers`, `/antibody-id?manufacturer=`) so a selected-cell vendor is findable, and show manufacturer on selected-cell pickers and the named inactive/expired lot on the open workup. The open worklist and dashboard summary show the next bench action (`NextAction`: record reactions, interpret, or supervisor review). The patient workup list shows Completed or Voided when `NextAction` is `None`. Dashboard names withdrawn or expired lots (`InactiveLotNumbers` / `ExpiredLotNumbers`) without identifying antibodies. Completing with active antibody history warns (`ABID-HIST-REMAINS`) and does not remove history; a current exclusion of a historical specificity warns (`ABID-HIST-UNDETECTED`). An open in-scope workup HardStops free-text ABID verify or a blood-attribute antibody verify that would post or deactivate history; a completed in-scope workup skips free-text or blood-attribute antibody history changes and warns (`ABID-WORKUP-AUTHORITATIVE` / `ABID-WORKUP-DISAGREE`). Rejecting a linked specimen warns (`ABID-SPEC-OPEN`), withdraws interpretation and review, still rejects, and keeps the open workup as the patient-wide identification of record until void or re-link. Opening another workup while that rejected or cancelled link remains HardStops (`ABID-WORKUP-DUP-OPEN`); re-linking the same workup is allowed. The open worklist, Antibodies card, and dashboard flag those unusable specimens (`HasUnusableSpecimen`, `UnusableSpecimenCount`). A clock-expired or expired-status linked specimen is also patient-wide for history post and new workups (`HasExpiredSpecimen`, `ExpiredSpecimenCount`); complete remains allowed with acknowledgment. A Received (not yet accepted) linked specimen is likewise patient-wide (`HasUnacceptedSpecimen`, `UnacceptedSpecimenCount`, OCD-031); complete remains Warning + acknowledgment (OCD-026). A Collected (not yet received) linked specimen is likewise patient-wide (`HasNotReadySpecimen`, `NotReadySpecimenCount`, OCD-032); complete and Accept remain HardStop (OCD-026). Dashboard and worklist specimen-attention badges open `/antibody-id?unusable=1`, `/antibody-id?expired=1`, `/antibody-id?unaccepted=1`, or `/antibody-id?notready=1` (`MatchesSpecimenAttention`). Next-action counts open `/antibody-id?action=reactions`, `?action=interpret`, or `?action=review` (`MatchesNextAction`). Dashboard inactive/expired lot counts and the worklist lot-attention count open `/antibody-id?lotAttention=1` (`MatchesLotAttention`); named lot badges still open `/antibody-id?lot=`. Withdrawn-judgment counts open `/antibody-id?withdrawn=1` (`MatchesWithdrawnJudgment`, `HasWithdrawnJudgment`). Pending type-correction counts open `/antibody-id?typePending=1` (`HasPendingTypeCorrection`, OCD-029). Reserved or issued unit counts open `/antibody-id?products=1` (`HasReservedOrIssuedUnits`); complete still warns and requires acknowledgment (`ABID-COMPLETE-PRODUCTS`). Editing collection or expiration warns (`ABID-SPEC-EDIT`) and withdraws interpretation. Adding or deactivating antibody history from the patient chart HardStops while any open workup exists for that patient (`ABID-WORKUP-OPEN`); the Antibodies card disables Add antibody and Deactivate. Deactivating a specificity posted by a completed workup warns (`ABID-DEACT-POSTED`) and still deactivates. Changing a patient antigen while a workup is open — chart save or a verified antigen result — warns (`ABID-AG-OPEN`), still records, and withdraws interpretation and review so they are repeated against the current type. Invalidating that verified antigen result warns (`ABID-AG-INVAL`) and withdraws again; phenotype is not auto-reverted. Manually changing ABO/Rh, or verifying an ABO/Rh result that changes current type, while a workup is open warns (`ABID-ABO-OPEN`), still records, and withdraws interpretation and review. Invalidating that verified ABO/Rh result warns (`ABID-ABO-INVAL`) and withdraws again; posted blood-type history is not auto-reverted. Correcting a verified ABO/Rh or antigen result while a workup is open warns (`ABID-TYPE-CORR`) and does not withdraw; complete and supervisor Accept HardStop (`ABID-TYPE-PENDING`) until it is verified or invalidated. A scoped workup HardStops only for a pending correction on the linked specimen. Changing antigen or ABO/Rh after a completed in-scope workup warns (`ABID-AG-DONE` / `ABID-ABO-DONE`) and does not reopen or retract posted history (same-specimen verify; chart save when the completed workup was unscoped, or when a posted Identified specificity conflicts with a new antigen-positive type). Adding or deactivating a special requirement while a workup is open warns (`ABID-SR-OPEN`) and still records. Allocating a unit while a workup is open warns (`ABID-ALLOC-OPEN`) and still reserves. Serologic XM warns (`ABID-XM-OPEN`) and still records. Issue warns (`ABID-ISSUE-OPEN`) after the safety gate and still issues without an extra override. Void requires a reason, posts nothing, and is blocked after complete. Expired/inactive lots HardStop new workups and additional lot attach. A deactivated attached lot HardStops Accept and complete; a clock-expired attached lot warns and requires acknowledgment. Designated staff create manufacturers (`ABID-MFG-CREATE-PERM`) and lots with cells and typed antigens (`ABID-LOT-CREATE-PERM`); create does not identify antibodies. Staff can review a lot's cell/antigen map and see open workups and completed identifications of record that used the lot or manufacturer (including patient MRN, posted-history count, and list-row `PostedHistoryWorkupCount`) without identifying antibodies. Voided workups are omitted. Staff can open the `/antibody-id` worklist filtered by lot (`?lot=`). A deactivated manufacturer cannot be used for a new lot. Designated staff deactivate or restore lots (`ABID-LOT-DEACT-PERM` / `ABID-LOT-ACT-PERM`); deactivation requires a reason and does not identify antibodies. Opening without a specimen warns (`ABID-WORKUP-UNSCOPED`). An open workup can link a specimen; completed or voided workups HardStop (`ABID-WORKUP-SPECIMEN`). A second overlapping open workup HardStops (`ABID-WORKUP-DUP-OPEN`). Rejected, cancelled, expired, or collected-not-received specimens HardStop create/link (`ABID-WORKUP-SPEC-UNUSABLE` / `ABID-WORKUP-SPEC-EXPIRED` / `ABID-WORKUP-SPEC-NOT-READY`). Received-not-accepted warns (`ABID-WORKUP-SPEC-UNACCEPTED`). Completing on a rejected, cancelled, or collected linked specimen HardStops; completing after the specimen expires or is still only received warns and requires acknowledgment. Dosage `CannotExclude` warns that a homozygous selected cell is needed (`ABID-SEL-CELL`). | URS-BB-063 | `AntibodyIdentificationAssistEvaluator`; `AntibodyIdentificationInterpretationRule`; `AntibodyIdentificationHistoryPostRule`; `AntibodyIdentificationService`; `ResultService` | `AntibodyIdentificationAssistEvaluatorTests`; `AntibodyIdentificationInterpretationRuleTests`; `AntibodyIdentificationHistoryPostRuleTests`; `AntibodyIdentificationWorkupTests`; `AntibodyIdentificationTests` |

A blank value, an unknown direction, or more than three columns is reported by line number and skipped; repeats (case-insensitive) are counted once. Translations page **Import rows** (paste or a CSV/TSV/TXT file up to 1 MB; add to or replace the table) puts the rows in the table only, prefilling the reason; Save applies the usual value-list checks, reason, history, and audit. **Export CSV** downloads the table as shown (`CsvFormat`, columns Local value, External value, Direction), which Import reads back unchanged. |

| FRS-BB-230 | Staff with `report.run` can run and export the operational report catalog at `/reports` to PDF or Excel. Staff with `report.design` design reports only under Administration at `/admin/reports`: a visual report (filter, group, aggregate) or one SELECT against `rpt_*` views. The visual designer includes a lab-results source (`rpt_lab_results`) with patient, specimen, test, and result fields; that source is not a seeded system report. System reports cannot be edited or deleted. Run and export write an audit event. The eight seeded reports are a facility starter set pending regulatory review, not an FDA, AABB, or CAP claim, and they do not record a reviewer sign-off. | URS-BB-195 | `ReportService`; `ReportSqlGuard`; `VisualReportCompiler`; `TabularReportRenderer` | `ReportSqlGuardTests`; `VisualReportCompilerTests`; `ReportServiceTests`; `TabularReportRendererTests` |
